### Węzeł `[24]`
Mówca: **Tuomas Sacker**
> ""Just a few days after the incident, Sordish anti-air guns shot down a Rumburgian airplane. This time, your government proclaimed exactly the same while the Rumburgian government insisted that the plane was inside their airspace.""
- **Połączenia wychodzące (`outgoingLinks`):**
  - -> `[26]`

### Węzeł `[25]`
Mówca: **Tuomas Sacker**
> ""Your government has been silent apart from a few statements which claimed that your plane was indeed in Sordish airspace when it was shot down. Do you believe the Rumburgian forces initiated an intentional attack?""
- **Połączenia wychodzące (`outgoingLinks`):**
  - -> `[31]`
  - -> `[32]`
  - -> `[33]`

> ""Many argue that this was not a coincidence and the Sordish army acted intentionally in retaliation. Did you give such an order?""

  - -> `[28]`
  - -> `[29]`
  - -> `[30]`

  - -> `[40]`
  - -> `[42]`
